- **al-multitenancy-reviewer**: Use this agent to audit a Business Central AL extension (and any companion API) for multi-tenant safety. The failure mode is a query that returns the wrong tenant's data, an endpoint that resolves a record by primary key without scoping the tenant, or a job-queue codeunit that processes rows from the wrong company. These bugs do not surface in single-tenant dev sandboxes; they show up in SaaS as cross-tenant data leak. - **al-obsolete-tracker**: Use this agent to audit `Obsolete` markings on a Business Central AL extension. Every object, field, procedure, or enum value marked `ObsoleteState = Pending` should have a clear `ObsoleteReason`, an `ObsoleteTag` carrying a target removal version, and a planned removal path. This agent reports orphans (Pending with no clear plan), broken removals (Removed without a prior Pending cycle), and abandoned obsolescence (Pending for many versions with no progress). - **amg-builder**: Semantic derivation worker for AMG bootstrap/reconcile. Given a batch of changed or new source units (from .claude/amg/work/queue.json), read each unit and write a concise summary plus meaning-bearing edges, emitting a derivation JSON file for the driver to apply. Use during `amg-bootstrap` step 3, one instance per batch, in parallel. Bulk, routine work — mid-tier model. ([Kvazar123/llm-amg](https://github.com/Kvazar123/llm-amg/blob/HEAD/agents/amg-builder.md) · ★8)
- **amg-classifier**: Resolve the content type of ambiguous files during AMG bootstrap. The deterministic classifier in extract_structure.py handles ~all files by extension and content signature; this subagent is invoked ONLY for the few it flags as ambiguous (extensionless files, unknown extensions, or text whose kind is unclear) so they route to the right chunker instead of the prose default. Light, cheap work. ([Kvazar123/llm-amg](https://github.com/Kvazar123/llm-amg/blob/HEAD/agents/amg-classifier.md) · ★8)
- **amg-consolidator**: Judgment pass for AMG memory maintenance, run during `amg-consolidate`. Reads the consolidation plan and relevant node bodies and decides what to promote, merge, summarize, group under a sub-hub, shorten, or retire — applying the value-of- information rubric and respecting branch budgets and protected types. Emits an actions JSON for the driver to apply transactionally. Decision-heavy — strong model. ([Kvazar123/llm-amg](https://github.com/Kvazar123/llm-amg/blob/HEAD/agents/amg-consolidator.md) · ★8)
- **amg-criterio**: Writes the `reference` — the judging criterion — for one candidate AMG scenario, from the literal user message and the answer key. Blind to the agent's prompt and to why the message was written. Writes to a file and returns only an acknowledgement. Invoked by the amg-escenarios skill; never invoke it for anything else. ([AndresMuelas2004/amg-harness](https://github.com/AndresMuelas2004/amg-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/amg-criterio.md) · ★1)
- **amg-investigador**: Researches how real people write to food-diary apps, from public sources, as input to AMG's scenario generation. Has the web and CANNOT read the repository — that blindness is the design. Writes its report to a file and returns only an acknowledgement. Invoked by the amg-escenarios skill; never invoke it for anything else. ([AndresMuelas2004/amg-harness](https://github.com/AndresMuelas2004/amg-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/amg-investigador.md) · ★1)
- **amg-juez**: Judges AMG run traces against the answer key and each scenario's reference. Writes verdicts to a file and returns only an acknowledgement. Deliberately blind to the agent's prompt and to which model produced the replies. Invoked by the amg skill; never invoke it for anything else. ([AndresMuelas2004/amg-harness](https://github.com/AndresMuelas2004/amg-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/amg-juez.md) · ★1)
- **amg-linker**: Global semantic linking pass for AMG, run during `amg-bootstrap` AFTER the hubs exist (post amg-synth). Given one candidate batch (.claude/amg/work/ link-batch-*.json — nodes plus their most similar neighbors, nominated over the WHOLE graph), confirm the candidates that are real relations, emit typed weighted cross-domain edges and section-&gt;hub memberships, and write a derivation JSON for the driver to apply. Bounded per-batch context, instances run in parallel — the global reach comes from the candidate nomination, not from a mega-context. Bulk confirmation work — mid-tier model. ([Kvazar123/llm-amg](https://github.com/Kvazar123/llm-amg/blob/HEAD/agents/amg-linker.md) · ★8)
- **amg-optimizador**: Given confirmed AMG failures, finds the root cause in the agent's prompt or tool descriptions and proposes exactly ONE atomic change. Writes the proposal to a file and returns an acknowledgement. Proposes only — it applies the change solely when its invocation orders it to, which happens in the amg skill's autonomous-loop mode. Invoked by the amg skill; never invoke it for anything else. ([AndresMuelas2004/amg-harness](https://github.com/AndresMuelas2004/amg-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/amg-optimizador.md) · ★1)
- **amg-refutador**: Attacks one candidate AMG scenario before it can become permanent — checks its figures, its capabilities, whether its criterion is judgeable, whether it is fair, and whether it contradicts the existing dataset. Finds problems and never fixes them. Writes to a file and returns only an acknowledgement. Invoked by the amg-escenarios skill; never invoke it for anything else. ([AndresMuelas2004/amg-harness](https://github.com/AndresMuelas2004/amg-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/amg-refutador.md) · ★1)
- **amg-retriever**: Read-only ISOLATED retriever for AMG. Given a query, assemble the context pack from the graph (query-biased Personalized PageRank) in an isolated context and return a short distilled answer plus the pack path — the pack itself stays out of the caller's window. Use when that isolation is the point (a summary question to the memory; an already-crowded main context); the default way to retrieve working context is the direct retrieve.py call (see the amg-retrieve skill). ([Kvazar123/llm-amg](https://github.com/Kvazar123/llm-amg/blob/HEAD/agents/amg-retriever.md) · ★8)
- **amg-retriever-fork**: Context-informed memory consult for AMG (Claude Code only). A FORK of the main session: it inherits the whole parent conversation, retrieves from the graph in its own window, and returns a distillate JUDGED AGAINST everything the session already knows — the pack itself never enters the parent window. Useful at every stage, not only late: early — a dense task briefing instead of importing a full pack; mid — the delta on a focus shift (only what the window does not already hold) and agreement/contradiction checks; late — revision of the session's work against memory. The default for ordinary wo… ([Kvazar123/llm-amg](https://github.com/Kvazar123/llm-amg/blob/HEAD/agents/amg-retriever-fork.md) · ★8)
- **amg-synth**: Global synthesis pass for AMG, run after structural + builder derivation during `amg-bootstrap` step 5. Produces top-level architecture/overview nodes, cross-domain edges (docs↔code), weighted multi-membership for cross-cutting topics, and a GAP REPORT (undocumented code, drifted docs, contradictions). This is the foundational, high-leverage pass — strongest model. ([Kvazar123/llm-amg](https://github.com/Kvazar123/llm-amg/blob/HEAD/agents/amg-synth.md) · ★8)
- **amg-voz**: Writes the literal user message for one AMG scenario, from a situation described in plain words. Deliberately blind to the dataset and to the answer key — it holds no Read tool at all, so it cannot see the register it exists to escape. Writes to a file and returns only an acknowledgement. Invoked by the amg-escenarios skill; never invoke it for anything else. ([AndresMuelas2004/amg-harness](https://github.com/AndresMuelas2004/amg-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/amg-voz.md) · ★1)
